## Corpus Check
- 3834 files · ~9,924,751 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.
- Unclassified: 84 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: (none) 28, .css 16, .entitlements 8)

## Summary
- 56820 nodes · 160189 edges · 1235 communities (1144 shown, 91 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 96% EXTRACTED · 4% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 5617 edges (avg confidence: 0.88)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `26082fe9`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
